     1	# adhd-unslop
     2	
     3	This repo is a plugin marketplace for Claude Code and Codex. Its main plugin
     4	combines [i-have-adhd](https://github.com/ayghri/i-have-adhd) and pstack's
     5	[unslop](https://github.com/michael-denyer/pstack-claude) with one
     6	tie-breaker.
     7	
     8	| Plugin | Skills | What it is |
     9	| --- | --- | --- |
    10	| `adhd-unslop` | `adhd-unslop`, `doctor` | The overlay rules plus both upstream texts, an always-on hook, and a doctor. Everything you need. |
    11	| `au-i-have-adhd` | `i-have-adhd` | Optional. A pinned, unchanged copy of ayghri/i-have-adhd |
    12	| `au-unslop` | `unslop` | Optional. A pinned, unchanged copy of the unslop skill from pstack-claude |
    13	
    14	All upstream text comes from copies pinned by commit and sha256 in
    15	`tools/upstream.json`. The `au-` prefix keeps the mirrors from clashing with
    16	the upstream plugins of the same name.
    17	
    18	## The rule
    19	
    20	Both rule sets stay active. When a rule from each would produce different text
    21	for the same passage:
    22	
    23	- In a direct reply to the user, i-have-adhd wins.
    24	- In all other writing, unslop wins. This includes files, docs, commit
    25	  messages, PR text, subagent prompts, and text you were asked to rewrite.
    26	
    27	Compatible rules apply everywhere. The skill defines both surfaces per
    28	passage, lists the known interactions in an outcome table, and adds
    29	independent off switches. The overlay text is in `src/adhd-unslop/overlay/`.
    30	
    31	## Install
    32	
    33	### Claude Code
    34	
    35	```bash
    36	claude plugin marketplace add rubenvarela/adhd-unslop
    37	claude plugin install adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop
    38	```
    39	
    40	Type `/adhd-unslop` in a session. If another command uses that name, type
    41	`/adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop`. The skill carries both upstream texts itself. It
    42	has `disable-model-invocation: true`, so nothing applies until you invoke it
    43	or turn on always-on mode.
    44	
    45	### Codex
    46	
    47	```bash
    48	codex plugin marketplace add rubenvarela/adhd-unslop --ref main
    49	codex plugin add adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop
    50	```
    51	
    52	Type `$adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop` in a session. Codex matches plugin skills by
    53	their full name, so a bare `$adhd-unslop` finds nothing.
    54	
    55	Do not link the skill into `~/.agents/skills`. Codex names a linked skill
    56	after the plugin that contains it, so the link becomes a second
    57	`adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop`, and with two skills of one name Codex loads
    58	neither. The doctor warns about such a link.
    59	
    60	### The optional mirrors
    61	
    62	Install `au-i-have-adhd` or `au-unslop` only if you want that skill on its
    63	own. `adhd-unslop` never needs them.
    64	
    65	```bash
    66	claude plugin install au-i-have-adhd@adhd-unslop     # then /au-i-have-adhd:i-have-adhd
    67	codex plugin add au-unslop@adhd-unslop               # then $au-unslop:unslop
    68	```
    69	
    70	They behave exactly as upstream ships them: i-have-adhd runs only when you
    71	type it, and unslop can also be chosen by the model. With the unslop mirror
    72	installed, Codex sometimes loads it on its own. That copy is separate from
    73	`adhd-unslop`, so `stop unslop` does not turn it off. If you use
    74	`adhd-unslop`, you do not need the unslop mirror.
    75	
    76	## Update
    77	
    78	Each plugin updates on its own.
    79	
    80	```bash
    81	claude plugin marketplace update adhd-unslop
    82	claude plugin update adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop
    83	
    84	codex plugin marketplace upgrade adhd-unslop
    85	codex plugin add adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop
    86	```
    87	
    88	Update a mirror the same way if you installed one.
    89	
    90	### From 0.2.x
    91	
    92	0.2.x installed the two mirrors as dependencies of `adhd-unslop`. 0.3.0 does
    93	not need them.
    94	
    95	- Claude Code: run the update commands above, then `claude plugin prune` to
    96	  remove the mirrors that 0.2.x installed for you. A mirror you installed by
    97	  name stays; update it with `claude plugin update au-unslop@adhd-unslop` or
    98	  `claude plugin update au-i-have-adhd@adhd-unslop`.
    99	- Codex: run the update commands above. Your hook trust carries over, because
   100	  the hook definitions did not change. Remove the mirrors with `codex plugin
   101	  remove au-i-have-adhd@adhd-unslop` and `codex plugin remove
   102	  au-unslop@adhd-unslop` if you installed them only for `adhd-unslop`.
   103	
   104	## Always-on mode
   105	
   106	Create a flag file to deliver the rules at session start without invoking
   107	the skill:
   108	
   109	```bash
   110	touch ~/.claude/.adhd-unslop-always      # or "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/.adhd-unslop-always"
   111	touch ~/.codex/.adhd-unslop-always       # or "$CODEX_HOME/.adhd-unslop-always"
   112	```
   113	
   114	Either flag turns always-on on in both runtimes. Remove every flag to stop.
   115	
   116	`ADHD_UNSLOP_ALWAYS` overrides the flags for one process, which helps in CI
   117	and scripted runs:
   118	
   119	- `ADHD_UNSLOP_ALWAYS=0`, `false`, or `off` turns it off even when a flag
   120	  exists.
   121	- `ADHD_UNSLOP_ALWAYS=1`, `true`, or `on` turns it on without a flag.
   122	- Any other value is ignored, and the doctor warns about it.
   123	
   124	Codex runs plugin hooks only after you review and trust them:
   125	
   126	1. Open `/hooks` in an interactive Codex session.
   127	2. Trust the `adhd-unslop` handlers.
   128	3. Restart.
   129	
   130	### When the rules arrive
   131	
   132	The hook runs at `startup`, `clear`, `compact`, and `resume`.
   133	
   134	- Claude Code keeps the earlier copy in a resumed session, so the hook adds
   135	  nothing on resume. If you turn always-on on in the middle of a session,
   136	  start a new session to get the rules.
   137	- Codex also keeps the earlier copy, but it can lose it: after a manual
   138	  compaction, Codex fires the hook only when your next message starts. If
   139	  you compact and quit before sending a message, the resumed session would
   140	  have no copy. So the hook injects on every Codex resume, which can leave a
   141	  second copy in context.
   142	- If the rules ever seem missing, invoke `/adhd-unslop` or
   143	  `$adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop`, or run the doctor. Invoking it while
   144	  always-on already delivered the rules adds a second, identical copy.
   145	
   146	### How the always-on hook works
   147	
   148	The hook carries its own copy of both upstream texts, so it works without the
   149	mirrors and without the model reading any file first.
   150	
   151	Claude Code caps each hook's output at 10,000 characters, and Codex allows
   152	about 2,500 tokens per handler by default. The rules are about 21,500
   153	characters, so the hook delivers them as three chunks from three handlers
   154	under one matcher:
   155	
   156	1. Scope, surfaces, precedence, and lifecycle.
   157	2. The i-have-adhd body, verbatim.
   158	3. The unslop body, verbatim, plus the final check.
   159	
   160	Every chunk has the same header, with its index and the first 12
   161	hexadecimal characters of the bundle's SHA-256. The header states that a
   162	bundle counts only when all three chunks with that id arrive, each with its
   163	END line, and that arriving instructions do not change mode state.
   164	
   165	Each handler sets `additionalContextLimit: 5000` for Codex. The launcher
   166	checks each chunk hash against `hooks/chunks/manifest.json` and checks the
   167	assembled size against both caps before printing. With always-on on and a
   168	broken install, it prints one JSON `systemMessage` with no context. With
   169	always-on off, it prints nothing.
   170	
   171	## Doctor
   172	
   173	Type `/adhd-unslop:doctor` or `$adhd-unslop:doctor`. It prints one OK, WARN,
   174	or FAIL line per check, with the fix for each problem, and changes nothing.
   175	It checks the install, the optional mirrors, the always-on flags and switch,
   176	the hook chunks, the Codex hooks feature, and a `~/.agents/skills` name
   177	clash. You can also run it directly:
   178	
   179	```bash
   180	node ~/.claude/plugins/cache/adhd-unslop/adhd-unslop/<version>/skills/doctor/scripts/doctor.mjs
   181	```
   182	
   183	## Switches
   184	
   185	- `stop adhd mode` turns the ADHD rules off. unslop stays as it was.
   186	- `stop unslop` turns the unslop rules off. ADHD stays as it was.
   187	- `normal mode` turns both off.
   188	- Invoking the skill again turns both on.
   189	
   190	Known limitations:
   191	
   192	- Mode state lives in the conversation. Compaction can lose it, and the
   193	  re-injected instructions then restore the active defaults. Repeat the stop
   194	  command if that happens.
   195	- Without always-on, Claude Code keeps an invoked skill through `/compact`
   196	  only up to 20,000 characters, and this skill is about 21,800, so the last
   197	  unslop rules and the final check drop out. After a resume and a compaction
   198	  it keeps none of it. For long sessions that compact, turn on always-on,
   199	  which delivers the whole rule set again at every compaction, or invoke the
   200	  skill again after compacting.
   201	
   202	## Upstream updates
   203	
   204	The `Bump upstream skills` workflow runs daily. It first runs
   205	`node tools/sync.mjs --verify-remote`, which refetches each pinned file and
   206	compares its hash. Then `node tools/sync.mjs --latest`:
   207	
   208	1. Finds the newest upstream commit that touched a pinned file.
   209	2. Skips an upstream when that commit changed none of the pinned files.
   210	3. Fetches the files and refuses a body that references files the plugins
   211	   do not ship, such as `references/`, `scripts/`, or relative links.
   212	4. Refuses a body that drops a rule, exception, check, or process step the
   213	   overlay cites.
   214	5. Swaps the files in, raises the patch version of each plugin that ships
   215	   the text, rebuilds, and runs the tests. It restores everything if a step
   216	   fails.
   217	
   218	The workflow opens or updates one PR with the results and starts the verify
   219	workflow on it. When a bump fails, it opens an issue with the reason. The
   220	usual cause is an upstream change to a rule the overlay cites, which needs an
   221	overlay edit. It needs the repository setting "Allow GitHub Actions to create
   222	and approve pull requests".
   223	
   224	To bump by hand:
   225	
   226	```bash
   227	node tools/sync.mjs --check                        # upstream/ matches the pins
   228	node tools/sync.mjs --bump i-have-adhd <commit>    # or: --bump unslop <commit>
   229	git diff
   230	```
   231	
   232	Review the upstream diff for conflicts with the other skill. Record them in
   233	the outcome table in `src/adhd-unslop/overlay/10-precedence.md`.
   234	
   235	## Editing
   236	
   237	Edit only `src/`, `tools/plugins.json`, and `tools/upstream.json`. Then:
   238	
   239	```bash
   240	node tools/build.mjs
   241	node --test tests/*.test.mjs
   242	```
   243	
   244	`node tools/build.mjs` generates everything under `plugins/` and both
   245	marketplace files. `node tools/build.mjs --check` fails when a generated file
   246	is stale or when a stray file sits under a generated folder, and
   247	`node tools/build.mjs --prune` deletes strays. Raise a plugin's version in
   248	`tools/plugins.json` whenever its shipped files change. CI enforces this.
   249	
   250	To add a plugin that uses i-have-adhd:
   251	
   252	1. Add an entry to `tools/plugins.json` with `"kind": "authored"` and
   253	   `"skills": { "<skill>": { "upstreams": ["i-have-adhd"] } }`.
   254	2. Write `src/<plugin>/skills/<skill>/SKILL.md.tmpl` with a line
   255	   `{{upstream i-have-adhd}}` where the rules belong, and
   256	   `agents/openai.yaml` for its Codex policy. The build puts the pinned
   257	   i-have-adhd text in place of that line.
   258	3. Build and test. Give the plugin no always-on hook of its own, or it would
   259	   inject the ADHD rules a second time.
   260	
   261	## Checks
   262	
   263	- `verify.yml` runs on every pull request and push to `main`: the pin check,
   264	  the generated-file check, the unit tests, a version gate on pull requests,
   265	  a keyless load check that installs the plugins into pinned Claude Code and
   266	  Codex versions, and workflow linting.
   267	- `cli-drift.yml` runs the load check daily against the latest CLI releases.
   268	  It opens an issue when that fails, and a PR that raises the pins in
   269	  `tools/cli-versions.json` when newer releases pass.
   270	- `tests/e2e/run.sh` drives the real models in throwaway homes. It needs both
   271	  CLIs signed in, so it runs locally. `tests/e2e/run.sh migrate` checks the
   272	  upgrade from 0.2.2 in both runtimes. `E2E_REPO=<owner/repo>
   273	  E2E_REF=<branch>` installs from GitHub.
   274	
   275	## Layout
   276	
   277	```
   278	.claude-plugin/marketplace.json     GENERATED Claude Code marketplace
   279	.agents/plugins/marketplace.json    GENERATED Codex marketplace
   280	plugins/                            GENERATED, every file
   281	  adhd-unslop/                      overlay skill, doctor, always-on hook
   282	  au-i-have-adhd/                   pinned copy of i-have-adhd
   283	  au-unslop/                        pinned copy of unslop
   284	src/adhd-unslop/                    hand-written sources
   285	  overlay/                          overlay sections
   286	  skills/                           skill templates, doctor, Codex metadata
   287	  hooks/                            hooks.json, launcher, shared helpers
   288	upstream/                           upstream files at the pinned commits
   289	tools/                              build.mjs, sync.mjs, load-check.mjs, version-gate.mjs,
   290	                                    plugins.json, upstream.json, cli-versions.json
   291	tests/                              node:test suite, fixtures, and tests/e2e/
   292	design/DECISIONS.md                 decisions, reasons, and verified platform facts
   293	design/                             plans, research, review rounds, and test runs
   294	AGENTS.md                           rules for agents; Claude Code and Codex both read it
   295	```
   296

   298	
   299	MIT. Both upstreams are MIT; see `NOTICE.md`.

     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	// Generate every shipped file from tools/plugins.json, tools/upstream.json,
     3	// upstream/, and src/. Nothing under plugins/ is edited by hand.
     4	//
     5	// - both marketplace files, and each plugin's Claude Code and Codex manifests,
     6	//   README.md, NOTICE.md, and LICENSES/
     7	// - vendored plugins: the upstream SKILL.md byte for byte, plus agents/openai.yaml
     8	// - authored plugins: src/<plugin>/skills/ and src/<plugin>/hooks/ copied, each
     9	//   SKILL.md.tmpl rendered, and the always-on chunks with their manifest
    10	//
    11	//   node tools/build.mjs           write every generated file
    12	//   node tools/build.mjs --check   exit 1 if a generated file is stale or missing,
    13	//                                  or a stray file sits under a generated folder
    14	//   node tools/build.mjs --prune   write, then delete stray files
    15	
    16	import fs from "node:fs";
    17	import path from "node:path";
    18	import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
    19	import { sha256, stripFrontmatter, TOTAL_CHUNKS } from "../src/adhd-unslop/hooks/lib.mjs";
    20	
    21	export const repo = path.join(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
    22	// Folders whose every file the build owns.
    23	export const GENERATED_ROOTS = ["plugins", ".claude-plugin", ".agents/plugins"];
    24	// Files the operating system drops into folders; never shipped, never strays.
    25	const IGNORED_NAMES = new Set([".DS_Store"]);
    26	
    27	const read = (...p) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(repo, ...p), "utf8");
    28	const json = (value) => JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) + "\n";
    29	const capitalize = (s) => s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);
    30	const endsWithNewline = (s) => (s.endsWith("\n") ? s : s + "\n");
    31	
    32	export function loadPins() {
    33	  return JSON.parse(read("tools", "upstream.json")).upstreams;
    34	}
    35	
    36	export function loadConfig() {
    37	  return JSON.parse(read("tools", "plugins.json"));
    38	}
    39	
    40	export const vendoredPlugins = (config = loadConfig()) => config.plugins.filter((p) => p.kind === "vendored");
    41	export const authoredPlugins = (config = loadConfig()) => config.plugins.filter((p) => p.kind === "authored");
    42	
    43	// Every upstream whose text a plugin ships: its mirror, the upstreams its skills
    44	// embed, and its always-on chunks. sync.mjs raises the version of each such plugin.
    45	export function upstreamsShipped(plugin) {
    46	  const names = new Set();
    47	  if (plugin.kind === "vendored") names.add(plugin.upstream);
    48	  for (const skill of Object.values(plugin.skills ?? {})) for (const n of skill.upstreams ?? []) names.add(n);
    49	  for (const chunk of plugin.alwaysOn ?? []) for (const part of chunk) if (part.upstream) names.add(part.upstream);
    50	  return [...names];
    51	}
    52	
    53	export function markers(name, pins) {
    54	  const pin = pins[name];
    55	  return {
    56	    begin: `<!-- BEGIN upstream ${name} ${pin.repo}/${pin.files["SKILL.md"].path} @${pin.commit} -->\n`,
    57	    end: `<!-- END upstream ${name} -->\n`,
    58	  };
    59	}
    60	
    61	// The upstream block (design/STRUCTURE-v2.md P1): the BEGIN line, the upstream
    62	// SKILL.md without frontmatter and with a final newline, the END line. The same
    63	// bytes go into a skill that embeds the upstream and into the always-on chunks.
    64	export function upstreamBlock(name, pins) {
    65	  const { begin, end } = markers(name, pins);
    66	  return begin + endsWithNewline(stripFrontmatter(read("upstream", name, "SKILL.md"))) + end;
    67	}
    68	
    69	// True when upstream frontmatter sets disable-model-invocation: true.
    70	export function userOnly(skillText) {
    71	  const m = skillText.match(/^---\n([\s\S]*?)\n---\n/);
    72	  return Boolean(m && /^disable-model-invocation:\s*true\s*$/m.test(m[1]));
    73	}
    74	
    75	export function openaiYaml({ displayName, shortDescription, defaultPrompt, allowImplicit }) {
    76	  const lines = ["interface:", `  display_name: ${JSON.stringify(displayName)}`, `  short_description: ${JSON.stringify(shortDescription)}`];
    77	  if (defaultPrompt) lines.push(`  default_prompt: ${JSON.stringify(defaultPrompt)}`);
    78	  lines.push("", "policy:", `  allow_implicit_invocation: ${allowImplicit}`, "");
    79	  return lines.join("\n");
    80	}
    81	
    82	// Template syntax, one directive per line:
    83	//   {{include <path under src/<plugin>/>}}  inserts that file
    84	//   {{upstream <name>}}                     inserts the pinned upstream block
    85	export function templateUpstreams(text) {
    86	  return [...text.matchAll(/^\{\{upstream (.+)\}\}$/gm)].map((m) => m[1]);
    87	}
    88	
    89	export function renderTemplate(pluginName, text, pins = loadPins()) {
    90	  return text
    91	    .split("\n")
    92	    .map((line) => {
    93	      const inc = line.match(/^\{\{include (.+)\}\}$/);
    94	      if (inc) return read("src", pluginName, inc[1]).replace(/\n$/, "");
    95	      const up = line.match(/^\{\{upstream (.+)\}\}$/);
    96	      if (up) {
    97	        if (!pins[up[1]]) throw new Error(`${pluginName}: template embeds unpinned upstream ${up[1]}`);
    98	        return upstreamBlock(up[1], pins).replace(/\n$/, "");
    99	      }
   100	      return line;
   101	    })
   102	    .join("\n");
   103	}
   104	
   105	function walk(dir) {
   106	  const out = [];
   107	  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return out;
   108	  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   109	    if (IGNORED_NAMES.has(entry.name)) continue;
   110	    const abs = path.join(dir, entry.name);
   111	    if (entry.isDirectory()) out.push(...walk(abs));
   112	    else out.push(abs);
   113	  }
   114	  return out;
   115	}
   116	
   117	// The always-on chunks: each chunk is its parts joined by a blank line.
   118	export function composeChunks(plugin, pins) {
   119	  const chunks = (plugin.alwaysOn ?? []).map((parts) =>
   120	    parts
   121	      .map((part) => (part.upstream ? upstreamBlock(part.upstream, pins) : endsWithNewline(read("src", plugin.name, part.file))))
   122	      .join("\n"),
   123	  );
   124	  const body = chunks.join("");
   125	  const manifest = {
   126	    generatedBy: "tools/build.mjs",
   127	    compositeSha256: sha256(body),
   128	    chunks: chunks.map((c, i) => ({
   129	      index: i + 1,
   130	      file: `${i + 1}.md`,
   131	      sha256: sha256(c),
   132	      bytes: Buffer.byteLength(c, "utf8"),
   133	      utf16Units: c.length,
   134	    })),
   135	  };
   136	  return { chunks, body, manifest };
   137	}
   138	
   139	function claudePluginJson(plugin, config) {
   140	  return {
   141	    name: plugin.name,
   142	    version: plugin.version,
   143	    description: plugin.description,
   144	    author: config.author,
   145	    homepage: config.homepage,
   146	    repository: config.repository,
   147	    license: "MIT",
   148	    keywords: plugin.keywords ?? [],
   149	  };
   150	}
   151	
   152	function codexPluginJson(plugin, config, hasHooks) {
   153	  const i = plugin.codexInterface;
   154	  return {
   155	    name: plugin.name,
   156	    version: plugin.version,
   157	    description: plugin.description,
   158	    author: config.author,
   159	    license: "MIT",
   160	    keywords: plugin.keywords ?? [],
   161	    skills: "./skills/",
   162	    ...(hasHooks ? { hooks: "./hooks/hooks.json" } : {}),
   163	    interface: {
   164	      displayName: i.displayName,
   165	      shortDescription: i.shortDescription,
   166	      longDescription: i.longDescription,
   167	      developerName: config.author.name,
   168	      category: capitalize(plugin.category),
   169	      capabilities: i.capabilities ?? ["Instructions"],
   170	      ...(i.defaultPrompt ? { defaultPrompt: i.defaultPrompt } : {}),
   171	      ...(i.brandColor ? { brandColor: i.brandColor } : {}),
   172	    },
   173	  };
   174	}
   175	
   176	function claudeMarketplace(config) {
   177	  // Entries carry no version: plugin.json holds it, and the docs say not to set both.
   178	  return {
   179	    $schema: "https://www.schemastore.org/claude-code-marketplace.json",
   180	    name: config.marketplace.name,
   181	    description: config.marketplace.description,
   182	    owner: config.marketplace.owner,
   183	    plugins: config.plugins.map((p) => ({
   184	      name: p.name,
   185	      description: p.marketplaceDescription ?? p.description,
   186	      source: `./plugins/${p.name}`,
   187	      category: p.category,
   188	    })),
   189	  };
   190	}
   191	
   192	function codexMarketplace(config) {
   193	  return {
   194	    name: config.marketplace.name,
   195	    interface: { displayName: config.marketplace.displayName },
   196	    plugins: config.plugins.map((p) => ({
   197	      name: p.name,
   198	      source: { source: "local", path: `./plugins/${p.name}` },
   199	      policy: { installation: "AVAILABLE", authentication: "ON_INSTALL", products: ["CODEX"] },
   200	      category: capitalize(p.category),
   201	    })),
   202	  };
   203	}
   204	
   205	function skillNames(plugin) {
   206	  return plugin.kind === "vendored" ? [plugin.skill] : Object.keys(plugin.skills ?? {});
   207	}
   208	
   209	function pluginReadme(plugin, config) {
   210	  const mkt = config.marketplace.name;
   211	  const id = `${plugin.name}@${mkt}`;
   212	  const skills = skillNames(plugin);
   213	  const lines = [
   214	    `# ${plugin.name}`,
   215	    "",
   216	    "<!-- GENERATED by tools/build.mjs from tools/plugins.json. -->",
   217	    "",
   218	    plugin.description,
   219	    "",
   220	    "## Install",
   221	    "",
   222	    "```bash",
   223	    `claude plugin marketplace add ${config.repository.replace("https://github.com/", "")}`,
   224	    `claude plugin install ${id}`,
   225	    "",
   226	    `codex plugin marketplace add ${config.repository.replace("https://github.com/", "")} --ref main`,
   227	    `codex plugin add ${id}`,
   228	    "```",
   229	    "",
   230	    "## Skills",
   231	    "",
   232	    ...skills.map((s) => `- \`/${plugin.name}:${s}\` in Claude Code, \`$${plugin.name}:${s}\` in Codex`),
   233	    "",
   234	    `See the repository README for details: ${config.homepage}`,
   235	    "",
   236	  ];
   237	  return lines.join("\n");
   238	}
   239	
   240	function noticeText(plugin, pins) {
   241	  const shipped = upstreamsShipped(plugin);
   242	  if (!shipped.length) return null;
   243	  const lines = [
   244	    "# Notice",
   245	    "",
   246	    plugin.kind === "vendored"
   247	      ? "This plugin ships an upstream skill file unchanged, byte for byte."
   248	      : "This plugin ships upstream skill text unchanged after removing its YAML frontmatter, in its skill and in the always-on hook chunks.",
   249	    "Each upstream keeps its own MIT license in `LICENSES/`.",
   250	    "",
   251	    "| Upstream | Repository | Commit | File | License file |",
   252	    "| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |",
   253	  ];
   254	  for (const name of shipped) {
   255	    const pin = pins[name];
   256	    lines.push(`| ${name} | https://github.com/${pin.repo} | ${pin.commit} | ${pin.files["SKILL.md"].path} | LICENSES/${name}.LICENSE |`);
   257	  }
   258	  if (shipped.includes("unslop")) lines.push("", "The unslop skill in pstack-claude is a port of Lauren Tan's pstack for Cursor.");
   259	  lines.push("");
   260	  return lines.join("\n");
   261	}
   262	
   263	export function expectedFiles() {
   264	  const config = loadConfig();
   265	  const pins = loadPins();
   266	  const files = new Map();
   267	  files.set(".claude-plugin/marketplace.json", json(claudeMarketplace(config)));
   268	  files.set(".agents/plugins/marketplace.json", json(codexMarketplace(config)));
   269	
   270	  for (const plugin of config.plugins) {
   271	    const root = `plugins/${plugin.name}`;
   272	    let hasHooks = false;
   273	
   274	    if (plugin.kind === "vendored") {
   275	      const text = read("upstream", plugin.upstream, "SKILL.md");
   276	      const skillDir = `${root}/skills/${plugin.skill}`;
   277	      files.set(`${skillDir}/SKILL.md`, text);
   278	      files.set(`${skillDir}/agents/openai.yaml`, openaiYaml({
   279	        displayName: plugin.codexInterface.displayName,
   280	        shortDescription: plugin.codexInterface.shortDescription,
   281	        allowImplicit: !userOnly(text),
   282	      }));
   283	    } else if (plugin.kind === "authored") {
   284	      const srcSkills = path.join(repo, "src", plugin.name, "skills");
   285	      const onDisk = fs.existsSync(srcSkills) ? fs.readdirSync(srcSkills).filter((d) => !IGNORED_NAMES.has(d)) : [];
   286	      const listed = Object.keys(plugin.skills ?? {});
   287	      const unlisted = onDisk.filter((d) => !listed.includes(d));
   288	      const missing = listed.filter((d) => !onDisk.includes(d));
   289	      if (unlisted.length || missing.length) {
   290	        throw new Error(`${plugin.name}: skills in tools/plugins.json and src/${plugin.name}/skills/ differ (unlisted: ${unlisted.join(", ") || "none"}; missing: ${missing.join(", ") || "none"})`);
   291	      }
   292	      for (const [skill, spec] of Object.entries(plugin.skills ?? {})) {
   293	        const srcDir = path.join(srcSkills, skill);
   294	        let embedded = [];
   295	        for (const abs of walk(srcDir)) {
   296	          const rel = path.relative(srcDir, abs).split(path.sep).join("/");
   297	          const out = `${root}/skills/${skill}/`;
   298	          if (rel === "SKILL.md.tmpl") {
   299	            const tmpl = fs.readFileSync(abs, "utf8");
   300	            embedded = templateUpstreams(tmpl);
   301	            files.set(out + "SKILL.md", renderTemplate(plugin.name, tmpl, pins));
   302	          } else {
   303	            files.set(out + rel, fs.readFileSync(abs, "utf8"));
   304	          }
   305	        }
   306	        const listed = spec.upstreams ?? [];
   307	        if (embedded.join() !== listed.join()) {
   308	          throw new Error(`${plugin.name}:${skill}: the template embeds [${embedded.join(", ")}] but tools/plugins.json lists upstreams [${listed.join(", ")}]`);
   309	        }
   310	      }
   311	      const srcHooks = path.join(repo, "src", plugin.name, "hooks");
   312	      for (const abs of walk(srcHooks)) {
   313	        hasHooks = true;
   314	        files.set(`${root}/hooks/${path.relative(srcHooks, abs).split(path.sep).join("/")}`, fs.readFileSync(abs, "utf8"));
   315	      }
   316	      if (plugin.alwaysOn) {
   317	        const { chunks, manifest } = composeChunks(plugin, pins);
   318	        if (chunks.length !== TOTAL_CHUNKS) throw new Error(`${plugin.name}: alwaysOn lists ${chunks.length} chunks; the launcher expects ${TOTAL_CHUNKS}`);
   319	        chunks.forEach((c, i) => files.set(`${root}/hooks/chunks/${i + 1}.md`, c));
   320	        files.set(`${root}/hooks/chunks/manifest.json`, json(manifest));
   321	      }
   322	    } else {
   323	      throw new Error(`${plugin.name}: unknown kind ${plugin.kind}`);
   324	    }
   325	
   326	    files.set(`${root}/.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, json(claudePluginJson(plugin, config)));
   327	    files.set(`${root}/.codex-plugin/plugin.json`, json(codexPluginJson(plugin, config, hasHooks)));
   328	    files.set(`${root}/README.md`, pluginReadme(plugin, config));
   329	    const notice = noticeText(plugin, pins);
   330	    if (notice) files.set(`${root}/NOTICE.md`, notice);
   331	    for (const up of upstreamsShipped(plugin)) files.set(`${root}/LICENSES/${up}.LICENSE`, read("upstream", up, "LICENSE"));
   332	  }
   333	  return files;
   334	}
   335	
   336	// Files under a generated folder that the build would not write.
   337	export function strayFiles(expected = expectedFiles()) {
   338	  const strays = [];
   339	  for (const top of GENERATED_ROOTS) {
   340	    for (const abs of walk(path.join(repo, top))) {
   341	      const rel = path.relative(repo, abs).split(path.sep).join("/");
   342	      if (!expected.has(rel)) strays.push(rel);
   343	    }
   344	  }
   345	  return strays.sort();
   346	}
   347	
   348	export function staleFiles(expected = expectedFiles()) {
   349	  const stale = [];
   350	  for (const [file, text] of expected) {
   351	    let current = null;
   352	    try { current = read(file); } catch { /* missing */ }
   353	    if (current !== text) stale.push(file);
   354	  }
   355	  return stale;
   356	}
   357	
   358	export function writeAll(expected = expectedFiles()) {
   359	  for (const [file, text] of expected) {
   360	    const abs = path.join(repo, file);
   361	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(abs), { recursive: true });
   362	    if (!fs.existsSync(abs) || fs.readFileSync(abs, "utf8") !== text) fs.writeFileSync(abs, text);
   363	  }
   364	}
   365	
   366	function removeEmptyDirs(dir) {
   367	  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return;
   368	  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   369	    if (entry.isDirectory()) removeEmptyDirs(path.join(dir, entry.name));
   370	  }
   371	  if (fs.readdirSync(dir).length === 0) fs.rmdirSync(dir);
   372	}
   373	
   374	export function prune(expected = expectedFiles()) {
   375	  const strays = strayFiles(expected);
   376	  for (const rel of strays) fs.rmSync(path.join(repo, rel));
   377	  for (const top of GENERATED_ROOTS) removeEmptyDirs(path.join(repo, top));
   378	  return strays;
   379	}
   380	
   381	if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
   382	  try {
   383	    const expected = expectedFiles();
   384	    if (process.argv.includes("--check")) {
   385	      const stale = staleFiles(expected);
   386	      const strays = strayFiles(expected);
   387	      if (stale.length) console.error("stale or missing generated files:\n  " + stale.join("\n  "));
   388	      if (strays.length) console.error("stray files under generated folders (run node tools/build.mjs --prune):\n  " + strays.join("\n  "));
   389	      if (stale.length || strays.length) process.exit(1);
   390	      console.log("generated files are current");
   391	    } else {
   392	      writeAll(expected);
   393	      console.log(`wrote ${expected.size} generated files`);
   394	      if (process.argv.includes("--prune")) {
   395	        for (const rel of prune(expected)) console.log(`deleted stray ${rel}`);
   396	      } else {
   397	        const strays = strayFiles(expected);
   398	        if (strays.length) console.warn("stray files under generated folders (delete with --prune):\n  " + strays.join("\n  "));
   399	      }
   400	    }
   401	  } catch (err) {
   402	    console.error(err.message);
   403	    process.exit(1);
   404	  }
   405	}

    26	const config = loadConfig();
    27	const pins = loadPins();
    28	const composed = config.plugins.find((p) => p.name === "adhd-unslop");
    29	const frontmatterOf = (text) => text.match(/^---\n([\s\S]*?)\n---\n/)[1];

    32	describe("generated files", () => {
    33	  test("every generated file matches a fresh build", () => {
    34	    assert.deepEqual(staleFiles(), []);
    35	  });
    36	
    37	  test("no stray files under generated folders", () => {
    38	    assert.deepEqual(strayFiles(), []);
    39	  });
    40	
    41	  test("a stray file is reported, and --prune removes only strays", () => {
    42	    const stray = path.join(repo, "plugins", "adhd-unslop", "skills", "adhd-unslop", "stray-test-file.md");
    43	    fs.writeFileSync(stray, "stray\n");
    44	    try {
    45	      assert.ok(strayFiles().includes("plugins/adhd-unslop/skills/adhd-unslop/stray-test-file.md"));
    46	      const removed = prune();
    47	      assert.deepEqual(removed, ["plugins/adhd-unslop/skills/adhd-unslop/stray-test-file.md"]);
    48	      assert.ok(!fs.existsSync(stray));
    49	      assert.deepEqual(staleFiles(), []);
    50	    } finally {
    51	      fs.rmSync(stray, { force: true });
    52	    }
    53	  });
    54	
    55	  test("no file under plugins/ is a symlink", () => {
    56	    const walk = (dir) => {
    57	      for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    58	        const abs = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    59	        assert.ok(!entry.isSymbolicLink(), `${path.relative(repo, abs)} is a symlink`);
    60	        if (entry.isDirectory()) walk(abs);
    61	      }
    62	    };
    63	    walk(path.join(repo, "plugins"));
    64	  });
    65	
    66	  test("plugins/ holds exactly the plugins in tools/plugins.json", () => {
    67	    const dirs = fs.readdirSync(path.join(repo, "plugins")).filter((d) => !d.startsWith(".")).sort();
    68	    assert.deepEqual(dirs, config.plugins.map((p) => p.name).sort());
    69	  });
    70	
    71	  test("every plugin ships at least one skill, and each listed skill ships a SKILL.md", () => {
    72	    for (const p of config.plugins) {
    73	      const names = skillNamesOf(p);
    74	      assert.ok(names.length > 0, `${p.name} has no skills`);
    75	      for (const s of names) assert.ok(fs.existsSync(path.join(repo, "plugins", p.name, "skills", s, "SKILL.md")), `${p.name}:${s}`);

    81	  });
    82

    84	    for (const [file, text] of expectedFiles()) {
    85	      if (file.endsWith(".md")) assert.doesNotMatch(text, /^\{\{include /m, file);

    91	describe("marketplaces and manifests", () => {

   141	    for (const p of config.plugins) assert.ok(!fs.existsSync(path.join(repo, "plugins", p.name, "plugin.json")), p.name);

   145	    for (const p of vendoredPlugins(config)) assert.notEqual(p.name, p.upstream);
   146	  });
   147	
   148	  test("each plugin ships a README, and a NOTICE and license for every upstream it ships", () => {
   149	    for (const p of config.plugins) {

   235	  test("skill size against Claude Code's 20,000-character re-attach budget after compaction", (t) => {
   236	    // Claude Code 2.1.283 re-attaches an invoked skill after /compact cut to
   237	    // 20,000 characters (design/research/11-tests-e2e.md). The skill is over
   238	    // it by design (DECISIONS D9); this keeps the gap visible in the output.
   239	    const over = skill.length - 20000;
   240	    t.diagnostic(`adhd-unslop SKILL.md is ${skill.length} characters, ${over > 0 ? `${over} over` : `${-over} under`} the re-attach budget`);

   249	  test("chunks on disk reproduce the bundle and match the manifest", () => {
   250	    const onDisk = manifest.chunks.map((m) => readPlugin("hooks", "chunks", m.file));
   251	    assert.equal(onDisk.length, TOTAL_CHUNKS);
   252	    assert.equal(onDisk.join(""), body);
   253	    onDisk.forEach((c, i) => {
   254	      assert.equal(c, chunks[i]);
   255	      assert.equal(sha256(c), manifest.chunks[i].sha256);
   256	      assert.equal(Buffer.byteLength(c, "utf8"), manifest.chunks[i].bytes);
   257	      assert.equal(c.length, manifest.chunks[i].utf16Units);
   258	    });
   259	    assert.equal(sha256(body), manifest.compositeSha256);
   260	    assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(readPlugin("hooks", "chunks", "manifest.json")), manifest);
   261	  });

   267	    assert.ok(chunks[1].startsWith(adhd.begin) && chunks[1].includes(adhd.end), "chunk 2 is the ADHD block");
   268	    assert.ok(chunks[2].startsWith(unslop.begin) && chunks[2].includes("## Final check"), "chunk 3 is unslop plus final check");
   269	  });

   306	describe("upstream pins", () => {
   307	  test("upstream/ files match tools/upstream.json sha256", () => {
   308	    for (const [name, pin] of Object.entries(pins)) {
   309	      for (const [file, meta] of Object.entries(pin.files)) assert.equal(sha256(read("upstream", name, file)), meta.sha256, `${name}/${file}`);
   310	      assert.match(pin.commit, /^[0-9a-f]{40}$/);
     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# Install this marketplace into throwaway Claude Code and Codex homes and check
     3	# the behavior with the real CLIs. Your own ~/.claude and ~/.codex are not
     4	# touched, except that ~/.codex/auth.json is copied into each throwaway Codex
     5	# home for the run. Every throwaway home, with its auth.json copy and Codex
     6	# shell_snapshots, is deleted on exit. Logs, transcripts, and rollouts stay in
     7	# the printed logs folder.
     8	#
     9	# Needs both CLIs signed in, and node and git, so CI does not run it. Uses the
    10	# working tree, including uncommitted changes. Run `node tools/build.mjs`
    11	# first, so plugins/ is current.
    12	#
    13	#   tests/e2e/run.sh            run everything
    14	#   tests/e2e/run.sh claude     fresh install in Claude Code
    15	#   tests/e2e/run.sh codex      fresh install in Codex
    16	#   tests/e2e/run.sh migrate    upgrade from 0.2.2 in both runtimes
    17	#
    18	# By default the marketplace is a copy of the working tree. To install from
    19	# GitHub instead, which exercises the real clone and cache paths:
    20	#
    21	#   E2E_REPO=rubenvarela/adhd-unslop E2E_REF=main tests/e2e/run.sh
    22	#
    23	# migrate always serves a local git marketplace over smart HTTP on 127.0.0.1.
    24	# Its main branch starts at 0.2.2 (commit fc308bd) and then moves to the
    25	# working tree, or to E2E_REF of E2E_REPO when that is set.
    26	#
    27	# PASS and FAIL lines are checks. INFO lines record platform behavior that is
    28	# kept on purpose or outside this repo's control, and never fail the run.
    29	#
    30	# CLAUDE_BIN and CODEX_BIN pick other CLI binaries, for example a newer Codex:
    31	#
    32	#   CODEX_BIN=/path/to/node_modules/.bin/codex tests/e2e/run.sh codex
    33	
    34	# Codex skill names such as $adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop are literal prompt text.
    35	# shellcheck disable=SC2016
    36	set -u
    37	repo=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.." && pwd)
    38	here=$repo/tests/e2e
    39	only=${1:-all}
    40	case $only in
    41	  all | claude | codex | migrate) ;;
    42	  *) echo "usage: $0 [claude|codex|migrate]"; exit 2 ;;
    43	esac
    44	
    45	claude_bin=${CLAUDE_BIN:-claude}
    46	codex_bin=${CODEX_BIN:-codex}
    47	real_home=$HOME
    48	# The commit that shipped adhd-unslop 0.2.2, the version migrate upgrades from.
    49	migrate_from=fc308bd
    50	# Hook trust keys of 0.2.2: three chunk handlers, then the dependency check.
    51	old_hook_keys=(0:0 0:1 0:2 1:0)
    52	# The three chunk handlers keep their keys, so their trust carries over.
    53	new_hook_keys=(0:0 0:1 0:2)
    54	
    55	# Drop the calling session's variables, so a run from inside Claude Code or
    56	# Codex behaves like one from a fresh terminal. Auth variables stay.
    57	for v in $(compgen -e); do
    58	  case $v in
    59	    CLAUDE_CODE_USE_* | CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN) ;;
    60	    CLAUDECODE | CLAUDE_CODE_* | CLAUDE_PID | CLAUDE_EFFORT | CLAUDE_PLUGIN_* | CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR | CLAUDE_SKILL_DIR) unset "$v" ;;
    61	    CODEX_HOME | CODEX_COMPANION_* | CODEX_THREAD_ID | PLUGIN_ROOT | AI_AGENT | ADHD_UNSLOP_*) unset "$v" ;;
    62	  esac
    63	done
    64	
    65	work=$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/adhd-unslop-e2e.XXXXXX")
    66	# Keep the model's working directory away from the marketplace copies, so a
    67	# file search cannot find an uninstalled plugin there.
    68	cwd=$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/adhd-unslop-cwd.XXXXXX")
    69	homes=$work/homes
    70	server_pid=
    71	cleanup() {
    72	  if [ -n "$server_pid" ]; then kill "$server_pid" 2>/dev/null; fi
    73	  # Delete the secrets first, then every throwaway home and marketplace copy,
    74	  # so no later run or model search finds their plugin copies.
    75	  find "$homes" \( -name auth.json -o -name 'logs_*.sqlite*' \) -exec rm -f {} + 2>/dev/null
    76	  find "$homes" -type d -name shell_snapshots -prune -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null
    77	  rm -rf "$cwd" "$homes" "$work/mkt" "$work/migrate"
    78	}
    79	trap cleanup EXIT
    80	trap 'exit 130' INT TERM HUP
    81	
    82	pass=0
    83	fail=0
    84	result() { if [ "$2" = ok ]; then echo "PASS  $1"; pass=$((pass + 1)); else echo "FAIL  $1"; fail=$((fail + 1)); fi; }
    85	expect() { local name=$1 pattern=$2 file=$3; if grep -Eq -- "$pattern" "$file" 2>/dev/null; then result "$name" ok; else result "$name" no; echo "      expected /$pattern/ in $file"; fi; }
    86	refuse() { local name=$1 pattern=$2 file=$3; if [ ! -f "$file" ]; then result "$name" no; echo "      missing $file"; elif grep -Eq -- "$pattern" "$file"; then result "$name" no; echo "      unexpected /$pattern/ in $file"; else result "$name" ok; fi; }
    87	# check NAME COMMAND...: PASS when COMMAND exits 0. Its output explains a FAIL.
    88	check() {
    89	  local name=$1 out
    90	  shift
    91	  if out=$("$@" 2>&1); then result "$name" ok; else result "$name" no; printf '%s\n' "$out" | sed 's/^/      /'; fi
    92	}
    93	# info NAME COMMAND...: an INFO line for platform behavior that is recorded,
    94	# not gated. It shows yes when COMMAND exits 0, no otherwise, and its output.
    95	info() {
    96	  local name=$1 out answer=no
    97	  shift
    98	  if out=$("$@" 2>&1); then answer=yes; fi
    99	  echo "INFO  $name: $answer"
   100	  if [ -n "$out" ]; then printf '%s\n' "$out" | sed 's/^/      /'; fi
   101	}
   102	inspect() { node "$here/inspect.mjs" "$@"; }
   103	
   104	# Plugin list helpers. FILE holds "id version [path]" lines from inspect plugins.
   105	has_plugin() {
   106	  local file=$1 id=$2 version=${3:-}
   107	  if awk -v id="$id" -v v="$version" '$1 == id && (v == "" || $2 == v) { found = 1 } END { exit !found }' "$file"; then return 0; fi
   108	  echo "expected $id${version:+ $version}; installed: $(awk '{ printf "%s %s; ", $1, $2 }' "$file")"
   109	  return 1
   110	}
   111	lacks_plugin() {
   112	  local file=$1 id=$2
   113	  if awk -v id="$id" '$1 == id { found = 1 } END { exit found }' "$file"; then return 0; fi
   114	  echo "unexpected $id; installed: $(awk '{ printf "%s %s; ", $1, $2 }' "$file")"
   115	  return 1
   116	}
   117	only_plugins() {
   118	  local file=$1 got want
   119	  shift
   120	  got=$(awk '$1 ~ /@adhd-unslop$/ { print $1 }' "$file" | sort | tr '\n' ' ')
   121	  want=$(printf '%s\n' "$@" | sort | tr '\n' ' ')
   122	  [ "$got" = "$want" ] && return 0
   123	  echo "installed from this marketplace: ${got:-nothing}; expected: $want"
   124	  return 1
   125	}
   126	only_entries() {
   127	  local dir=$1 got want
   128	  shift
   129	  got=$(find "$dir" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -exec basename {} \; 2>/dev/null | sort | tr '\n' ' ')
   130	  want=$(printf '%s\n' "$@" | sort | tr '\n' ' ')
   131	  [ "$got" = "$want" ] && return 0
   132	  echo "$dir holds: ${got:-nothing}; expected: $want"
   133	  return 1
   134	}
   135	keys_for() { local plugin=$1 k out=; shift; for k in "$@"; do out="$out $plugin:hooks/hooks.json:session_start:$k"; done; echo "$out"; }
   136	
   137	# Tree helpers.
   138	copy_tree() {
   139	  # Copy a checkout's tracked and untracked files, minus ignored and deleted ones.
   140	  (cd "$1" && git ls-files -co --exclude-standard -z | while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do
   141	    if [ -e "$f" ] || [ -L "$f" ]; then printf '%s\0' "$f"; fi
   142	  done | xargs -0 tar cf -) | (cd "$2" && tar xf -)
   143	}
   144	commit_all() { git -C "$1" add -A && git -C "$1" -c user.name=e2e -c user.email=e2e@example.invalid commit -qm "$2"; }
   145	json_field() { node -e 'const d=JSON.parse(require("fs").readFileSync(0,"utf8"));console.log(d[process.argv[1]])' "$1"; }
   146	bundle_from_manifest() { node -e 'console.log(JSON.parse(require("fs").readFileSync(0,"utf8")).compositeSha256.slice(0,12))'; }
   147	
   148	# Claude Code helpers. Each run gets its own config dir. CODEX_HOME points at
   149	# a missing folder, so a flag file in your real ~/.codex cannot turn
   150	# always-on on.
   151	claude_home=
   152	new_claude_home() { claude_home=$homes/$1; mkdir -p "$claude_home"; }
   153	cl() { CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=$claude_home CODEX_HOME=$claude_home/no-codex "$claude_bin" "$@" </dev/null; }
   154	# clp LOG ARGS...: one print-mode turn from $cwd, as stream-json with hook events.
   155	clp() {
   156	  local log=$1
   157	  shift
   158	  (cd "$cwd" && CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=$claude_home CODEX_HOME=$claude_home/no-codex \
   159	    "$claude_bin" -p --output-format stream-json --verbose --include-hook-events "$@" </dev/null >"$log" 2>&1)
   160	}
   161	# clturns LOG PROMPT...: several turns in one print-mode process from $cwd.
   162	clturns() {
   163	  local log=$1
   164	  shift
   165	  (cd "$cwd" && CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=$claude_home CODEX_HOME=$claude_home/no-codex \
   166	    node "$here/claude-turns.mjs" "$claude_bin" "$log" "$@" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1)
   167	}
   168	claude_session() { inspect session "$1" 2>/dev/null; }
   169	claude_transcript() { find "$claude_home/projects" -name "$1.jsonl" 2>/dev/null | head -n 1; }
   170	claude_plugins() { cl plugin list --json >"$1.json" 2>"$1.err"; inspect plugins claude "$1.json" >"$1"; }
   171	
   172	# Codex helpers. HOME and CODEX_HOME point at the throwaway home, so neither
   173	# ~/.claude nor ~/.codex flag files count. API keys are unset, so Codex
   174	# cannot write them into shell_snapshots.
   175	codex_home=
   176	new_codex_home() {
   177	  codex_home=$homes/$1
   178	  mkdir -p "$codex_home/.codex"
   179	  cp "$real_home/.codex/auth.json" "$codex_home/.codex/auth.json"
   180	}
   181	cx_env() { env -u ANTHROPIC_API_KEY -u OPENAI_API_KEY -u CODEX_API_KEY HOME="$codex_home" CODEX_HOME="$codex_home/.codex" "$@"; }
   182	cxc() { cx_env "$codex_bin" "$@" </dev/null; }
   183	# cxe LOG ARGS...: one codex exec turn from $cwd.
   184	cxe() {
   185	  local log=$1
   186	  shift
   187	  (cd "$cwd" && cx_env "$codex_bin" exec --skip-git-repo-check -s read-only "$@" </dev/null >"$log" 2>&1)
   188	}
   189	codex_session() { sed -n 's/^session id: //p' "$1" | head -n 1; }
   190	codex_rollout() { find "$codex_home/.codex/sessions" -name "rollout-*-$1.jsonl" 2>/dev/null | head -n 1; }
   191	codex_plugins() { cxc plugin list --json >"$1.json" 2>"$1.err"; inspect plugins codex "$1.json" >"$1"; }
   192	codex_hooks() { cx_env node "$here/codex-hooks.mjs" "$codex_bin" "$cwd" "$@"; }
   193	
   194	same_claude_session() {
   195	  local log=$1 sid=$2 transcript=$3 prompt=$4 got
   196	  got=$(claude_session "$log")
   197	  [ "$got" = "$sid" ] || { echo "resumed session is ${got:-missing}, expected $sid"; return 1; }
   198	  grep -Fq "$prompt" "$transcript" || { echo "transcript $transcript lacks the resumed prompt"; return 1; }
   199	}
   200	same_codex_session() {
   201	  local log=$1 sid=$2 rollout=$3 prompt=$4 got
   202	  got=$(codex_session "$log")
   203	  [ "$got" = "$sid" ] || { echo "resumed session is ${got:-missing}, expected $sid"; return 1; }
   204	  grep -Fq "$prompt" "$rollout" || { echo "rollout $rollout lacks the resumed prompt"; return 1; }
   205	}

   207	echo "logs: $work"
   208	echo "claude: $("$claude_bin" --version 2>&1 | head -n 1)"
   209	echo "codex: $("$codex_bin" --version 2>&1 </dev/null | head -n 1)"

   211	# Marketplace for the fresh-install runs. In local mode the expected versions
   212	# and bundle id come from the copy; in GitHub mode from the installed copy.
   213	source_ready=
   214	mkt=
   215	setup_source() {
   216	  [ -n "$source_ready" ] && return
   217	  source_ready=1
   218	  if [ -n "${E2E_REPO:-}" ]; then
   219	    ref=${E2E_REF:-main}
   220	    claude_source="$E2E_REPO#$ref"
   221	    codex_source=("$E2E_REPO" --ref "$ref")
   222	    echo "marketplace: $E2E_REPO at $ref"
   223	  else
   224	    mkt=$work/mkt
   225	    mkdir -p "$mkt"
   226	    copy_tree "$repo" "$mkt"
   227	    git -c init.defaultBranch=main init -q "$mkt"
   228	    commit_all "$mkt" e2e
   229	    claude_source=$mkt
   230	    codex_source=("$mkt")
   231	    echo "marketplace copy: $mkt"
   232	  fi
   233	}
   234	want_version() { [ -n "$mkt" ] && json_field version <"$mkt/plugins/$1/.claude-plugin/plugin.json"; }
   235	# The last line of the skill, which is the last line of the final check.
   236	want_final_line() {
   237	  local file
   238	  if [ -n "$mkt" ]; then file=$mkt/src/adhd-unslop/overlay/90-final-check.md; else file=$1/skills/adhd-unslop/SKILL.md; fi
   239	  awk 'NF { last = $0 } END { print last }' "$file" 2>/dev/null
   240	}
   241	want_bundle() {
   242	  local root=$1
   243	  if [ -n "$mkt" ]; then root=$mkt/plugins/adhd-unslop; fi
   244	  bundle_from_manifest <"$root/hooks/chunks/manifest.json" 2>/dev/null
   245	}
   246	
   247	run_claude() {
   248	  setup_source
   249	  new_claude_home claude
   250	  local log=$work/claude-install.log sid transcript bundle root final
   251	  cl plugin marketplace add "$claude_source" >"$log" 2>&1
   252	  cl plugin install adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop >>"$log" 2>&1
   253	  claude_plugins "$work/claude-plugins-1.txt"
   254	  check "claude: installing adhd-unslop installs only adhd-unslop" only_plugins "$work/claude-plugins-1.txt" adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop
   255	  check "claude: adhd-unslop has the expected version" has_plugin "$work/claude-plugins-1.txt" adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop "$(want_version adhd-unslop)"
   256	  refuse "claude: install reports no dependencies" "dependenc" "$log"
   257	  root=$(awk '$1 == "adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop" { print $3 }' "$work/claude-plugins-1.txt")
   258	  bundle=$(want_bundle "$root")
   259	  final=$(want_final_line "$root")
   260	  echo "      bundle id: ${bundle:-unknown}; final check line: ${final:-unknown}"
   261	
   262	  # Always-on is off: no flag file and ADHD_UNSLOP_ALWAYS unset.
   263	  log=$work/claude-invoke.log
   264	  clp "$log" '/adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop Reply with the single word READY.'
   265	  sid=$(claude_session "$log")
   266	  transcript=$(claude_transcript "$sid")
   267	  [ -n "$transcript" ] && cp "$transcript" "$work/claude-invoke.transcript.jsonl"
   268	  expect "claude: /adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop answers" '"result":"READY' "$log"
   269	  check "claude: always-on off injects no chunk" inspect bundle claude "$transcript" "$bundle" none
   270	  check "claude: skill text arrives with both upstream texts and the final check line" \
   271	    inspect skill-arrived claude "$transcript" "$root/skills/adhd-unslop/SKILL.md" "$final"
   272	  check "claude: skill body arrives whole" \
   273	    inspect skill-arrived claude "$transcript" "$root/skills/adhd-unslop/SKILL.md" "$final" whole
   274	
   275	  # The clean case: only adhd-unslop is installed.
   276	  log=$work/claude-unprompted.log
   277	  clp "$log" 'What is 17 times 3? Digits only.'
   278	  expect "claude: unprompted question answers" '"result":"51' "$log"
   279	  check "claude: unprompted question loads no skill" inspect no-skill "$log"
   280	
   281	  # Platform behavior, recorded as INFO: what an invoked skill keeps after
   282	  # compaction. The skill is larger than the 5,000-token re-attach budget.
   283	  log=$work/claude-compact-same.log
   284	  clturns "$log" '/adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop Reply with the single word READY.' '/compact'
   285	  transcript=$(claude_transcript "$(claude_session "$log")")
   286	  [ -n "$transcript" ] && cp "$transcript" "$work/claude-compact-same.transcript.jsonl"
   287	  info "claude: after /compact in the same process, the skill keeps its final check line" \
   288	    inspect compacted "$transcript" adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop "$final"
   289	  log=$work/claude-compact-resume.log
   290	  clp "$log" --resume "$sid" '/compact'
   291	  transcript=$(claude_transcript "$sid")
   292	  [ -n "$transcript" ] && cp "$transcript" "$work/claude-compact-resume.transcript.jsonl"
   293	  info "claude: after --resume then /compact, the skill keeps its final check line" \
   294	    inspect compacted "$transcript" adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop "$final"
   295	
   296	  log=$work/claude-install.log
   297	  cl plugin install au-i-have-adhd@adhd-unslop >>"$log" 2>&1
   298	  cl plugin install au-unslop@adhd-unslop >>"$log" 2>&1
   299	  claude_plugins "$work/claude-plugins-2.txt"
   300	  check "claude: au-i-have-adhd installs by name" has_plugin "$work/claude-plugins-2.txt" au-i-have-adhd@adhd-unslop "$(want_version au-i-have-adhd)"
   301	  check "claude: au-unslop installs by name" has_plugin "$work/claude-plugins-2.txt" au-unslop@adhd-unslop "$(want_version au-unslop)"
   302	
   303	  log=$work/claude-typed-adhd.log
   304	  clp "$log" '/au-i-have-adhd:i-have-adhd Reply with the number of the rule that caps list length, digits only.'
   305	  expect "claude: typed /au-i-have-adhd:i-have-adhd works" '"result":"9[^0-9]' "$log"
   306	
   307	  log=$work/claude-typed-unslop.log
   308	  clp "$log" '/au-unslop:unslop Reply with the number of the rule about em dashes, digits only.'
   309	  expect "claude: typed /au-unslop:unslop works" '"result":"13[^0-9]' "$log"
   310	
   311	  touch "$claude_home/.adhd-unslop-always"
   312	  log=$work/claude-always-on.log
   313	  clp "$log" 'Reply with the single word OK.'
   314	  sid=$(claude_session "$log")
   315	  transcript=$(claude_transcript "$sid")
   316	  [ -n "$transcript" ] && cp "$transcript" "$work/claude-always-on.transcript.jsonl"
   317	  check "claude: always-on delivers one complete bundle at startup" inspect bundle claude "$transcript" "$bundle" exact
   318	
   319	  log=$work/claude-resume.log
   320	  clp "$log" --resume "$sid" 'Reply with the single word AGAIN.'
   321	  check "claude: --resume continues the same session" same_claude_session "$log" "$sid" "$transcript" 'Reply with the single word AGAIN.'
   322	  expect "claude: resume fires SessionStart with source resume" '"hook_name":"SessionStart:resume"' "$log"
   323	  [ -n "$transcript" ] && cp "$transcript" "$work/claude-resume.transcript.jsonl"
   324	  check "claude: resumed transcript holds exactly one copy of each chunk" inspect bundle claude "$transcript" "$bundle" exact
   325	}
   326	
   327	run_codex() {
   328	  setup_source
   329	  new_codex_home codex
   330	  local log=$work/codex-install.log sid rollout bundle version root final
   331	  cxc plugin marketplace add "${codex_source[@]}" >"$log" 2>&1
   332	  cxc plugin add adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop >>"$log" 2>&1
   333	  codex_plugins "$work/codex-plugins-1.txt"
   334	  check "codex: installing adhd-unslop installs only adhd-unslop" only_plugins "$work/codex-plugins-1.txt" adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop
   335	  check "codex: adhd-unslop has the expected version" has_plugin "$work/codex-plugins-1.txt" adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop "$(want_version adhd-unslop)"
   336	  check "codex: plugin cache holds only adhd-unslop" only_entries "$codex_home/.codex/plugins/cache/adhd-unslop" adhd-unslop
   337	  version=$(awk '$1 == "adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop" { print $2 }' "$work/codex-plugins-1.txt")
   338	  root=$codex_home/.codex/plugins/cache/adhd-unslop/adhd-unslop/$version
   339	  bundle=$(want_bundle "$root")
   340	  final=$(want_final_line "$root")
   341	  echo "      bundle id: ${bundle:-unknown}; final check line: ${final:-unknown}"
   342	
   343	  # Always-on is off: no flag file, and the hooks are not trusted yet.
   344	  log=$work/codex-invoke.log
   345	  cxe "$log" -o "$work/codex-invoke.txt" '$adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop Reply with the single word READY.'
   346	  sid=$(codex_session "$log")
   347	  rollout=$(codex_rollout "$sid")
   348	  [ -n "$rollout" ] && cp "$rollout" "$work/codex-invoke.rollout.jsonl"
   349	  expect "codex: \$adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop answers" 'READY' "$work/codex-invoke.txt"
   350	  check "codex: always-on off injects no chunk" inspect bundle codex "$rollout" "$bundle" none
   351	  check "codex: skill text arrives with both upstream texts and the final check line" \
   352	    inspect skill-arrived codex "$rollout" "$root/skills/adhd-unslop/SKILL.md" "$final"
   353	  check "codex: skill body arrives whole, not cut" \
   354	    inspect skill-arrived codex "$rollout" "$root/skills/adhd-unslop/SKILL.md" "$final" whole
   355	
   356	  # The clean case: only adhd-unslop is installed.
   357	  log=$work/codex-unprompted.log
   358	  cxe "$log" -o "$work/codex-unprompted.txt" 'What is 17 times 3? Digits only.'
   359	  rollout=$(codex_rollout "$(codex_session "$log")")
   360	  expect "codex: unprompted question answers" '^51' "$work/codex-unprompted.txt"
   361	  refuse "codex: unprompted question reads no skill file" 'SKILL\.md' "$log"
   362	  refuse "codex: unprompted question injects no plugin skill" '<name>(adhd-unslop|au-i-have-adhd|au-unslop):' "$rollout"
   363	
   364	  log=$work/codex-install.log
   365	  cxc plugin add au-i-have-adhd@adhd-unslop >>"$log" 2>&1
   366	  cxc plugin add au-unslop@adhd-unslop >>"$log" 2>&1
   367	  codex_plugins "$work/codex-plugins-2.txt"
   368	  check "codex: au-i-have-adhd installs by name" has_plugin "$work/codex-plugins-2.txt" au-i-have-adhd@adhd-unslop "$(want_version au-i-have-adhd)"
   369	  check "codex: au-unslop installs by name" has_plugin "$work/codex-plugins-2.txt" au-unslop@adhd-unslop "$(want_version au-unslop)"
   370	
   371	  log=$work/codex-typed-adhd.log
   372	  cxe "$log" -o "$work/codex-typed-adhd.txt" '$au-i-have-adhd:i-have-adhd Reply with the number of the rule that caps list length, digits only.'
   373	  expect "codex: typed \$au-i-have-adhd:i-have-adhd works" '^9([^0-9]|$)' "$work/codex-typed-adhd.txt"
   374	
   375	  log=$work/codex-typed-unslop.log
   376	  cxe "$log" -o "$work/codex-typed-unslop.txt" '$au-unslop:unslop Reply with the number of the rule about em dashes, digits only.'
   377	  expect "codex: typed \$au-unslop:unslop works" '^13([^0-9]|$)' "$work/codex-typed-unslop.txt"
   378	
   379	  # With the mirrors installed. au-unslop keeps upstream's model-invocable
   380	  # "Must always apply" skill on purpose, so its auto-load is INFO.
   381	  log=$work/codex-unprompted-mirrors.log
   382	  cxe "$log" -o "$work/codex-unprompted-mirrors.txt" 'What is 17 times 3? Digits only.'
   383	  expect "codex: unprompted question with the mirrors answers" '^51' "$work/codex-unprompted-mirrors.txt"
   384	  refuse "codex: with the mirrors, the unprompted question loads no adhd-unslop or au-i-have-adhd skill" \
   385	    'cache/adhd-unslop/(adhd-unslop|au-i-have-adhd)/[^ ]*SKILL\.md' "$log"
   386	  info "codex: with the mirrors, the unprompted question auto-loads au-unslop:unslop" \
   387	    grep -Eo 'au-unslop/[^ ]*/SKILL\.md' "$log"
   388	
   389	  touch "$codex_home/.codex/.adhd-unslop-always"
   390	  log=$work/codex-always-on.log
   391	  cxe "$log" --dangerously-bypass-hook-trust -o "$work/codex-always-on.txt" 'Reply with the single word OK.'
   392	  sid=$(codex_session "$log")
   393	  rollout=$(codex_rollout "$sid")
   394	  [ -n "$rollout" ] && cp "$rollout" "$work/codex-always-on.rollout.jsonl"
   395	  check "codex: always-on delivers one complete bundle at startup" inspect bundle codex "$rollout" "$bundle" exact
   396	
   397	  log=$work/codex-resume.log
   398	  cxe "$log" --dangerously-bypass-hook-trust resume --last 'Reply with the single word AGAIN.'
   399	  check "codex: exec resume --last continues the same session" same_codex_session "$log" "$sid" "$rollout" 'Reply with the single word AGAIN.'
   400	  [ -n "$rollout" ] && cp "$rollout" "$work/codex-resume.rollout.jsonl"
   401	  check "codex: resumed rollout holds at least one copy of each chunk" inspect bundle codex "$rollout" "$bundle" atleast
   402	}
   403	
   404	# A local git marketplace whose main branch starts at 0.2.2 and moves to the
   405	# tree under test. Both runtimes reset main to 0.2.2 before they install.
   406	setup_migrate() {
   407	  local src=$work/migrate/src srv=$work/migrate/srv next=$work/migrate/next port
   408	  mkdir -p "$src" "$srv" "$next"
   409	  git -C "$repo" archive "$migrate_from" | tar xf - -C "$src" || return 1
   410	  git -c init.defaultBranch=main init -q "$src"
   411	  commit_all "$src" "adhd-unslop 0.2.2 ($migrate_from)"
   412	  old_commit=$(git -C "$src" rev-parse HEAD)
   413	  if [ -n "${E2E_REPO:-}" ]; then
   414	    git clone -q --depth 1 --branch "${E2E_REF:-main}" "https://github.com/$E2E_REPO.git" "$work/migrate/gh" || return 1
   415	    copy_tree "$work/migrate/gh" "$next"
   416	  else
   417	    copy_tree "$repo" "$next"
   418	  fi
   419	  git -C "$src" rm -rqf .
   420	  (cd "$next" && tar cf - .) | (cd "$src" && tar xf -)
   421	  commit_all "$src" "tree under test"
   422	  new_commit=$(git -C "$src" rev-parse HEAD)
   423	  git clone -q --bare "$src" "$srv/adhd-unslop.git"
   424	  bare=$srv/adhd-unslop.git
   425	  old_version=$(git -C "$src" show "$old_commit:plugins/adhd-unslop/.claude-plugin/plugin.json" | json_field version)
   426	  new_version=$(git -C "$src" show "$new_commit:plugins/adhd-unslop/.claude-plugin/plugin.json" | json_field version)
   427	  new_mirror_version=$(git -C "$src" show "$new_commit:plugins/au-unslop/.claude-plugin/plugin.json" | json_field version)
   428	  new_bundle=$(git -C "$src" show "$new_commit:plugins/adhd-unslop/hooks/chunks/manifest.json" | bundle_from_manifest)
   429	
   430	  node "$here/githttp.mjs" "$srv" >"$work/githttp.log" 2>&1 &
   431	  server_pid=$!
   432	  for _ in $(seq 1 100); do
   433	    grep -q '^listening' "$work/githttp.log" 2>/dev/null && break
   434	    sleep 0.1
   435	  done
   436	  port=$(awk '/^listening/ { print $2 }' "$work/githttp.log")
   437	  [ -n "$port" ] || return 1
   438	  migrate_url=http://127.0.0.1:$port/adhd-unslop.git
   439	  echo "migrate marketplace: $migrate_url, $old_version at ${old_commit:0:7} then $new_version at ${new_commit:0:7} (bundle $new_bundle)"
   440	}
   441	move_main() { git --git-dir="$bare" update-ref refs/heads/main "$1"; }
   442	
   443	migrate_claude() {
   444	  move_main "$old_commit"
   445	  new_claude_home claude-migrate
   446	  local log=$work/migrate-claude.log sid transcript
   447	  cl plugin marketplace add "$migrate_url" >"$log" 2>&1
   448	  cl plugin install adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop >>"$log" 2>&1
   449	  cl plugin install au-unslop@adhd-unslop >>"$log" 2>&1
   450	  touch "$claude_home/.adhd-unslop-always"
   451	  claude_plugins "$work/migrate-claude-before.txt"
   452	  check "migrate claude: $old_version installs" has_plugin "$work/migrate-claude-before.txt" adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop "$old_version"
   453	  check "migrate claude: $old_version pulls in au-i-have-adhd as a dependency" has_plugin "$work/migrate-claude-before.txt" au-i-have-adhd@adhd-unslop
   454	  check "migrate claude: au-unslop is installed" has_plugin "$work/migrate-claude-before.txt" au-unslop@adhd-unslop
   455	
   456	  move_main "$new_commit"
   457	  {
   458	    echo "== marketplace update"
   459	    cl plugin marketplace update adhd-unslop
   460	    echo "== plugin update"
   461	    cl plugin update adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop
   462	    echo "== prune"
   463	    cl plugin prune -y
   464	    echo "== update the kept mirror"
   465	    cl plugin update au-unslop@adhd-unslop
   466	  } >>"$log" 2>&1
   467	  claude_plugins "$work/migrate-claude-after.txt"
   468	  check "migrate claude: adhd-unslop updates to $new_version" has_plugin "$work/migrate-claude-after.txt" adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop "$new_version"
   469	  check "migrate claude: prune removes au-i-have-adhd" lacks_plugin "$work/migrate-claude-after.txt" au-i-have-adhd@adhd-unslop
   470	  check "migrate claude: au-unslop installed by name stays and updates to $new_mirror_version" has_plugin "$work/migrate-claude-after.txt" au-unslop@adhd-unslop "$new_mirror_version"
   471	
   472	  log=$work/migrate-claude-session.log
   473	  clp "$log" 'Reply with the single word OK.'
   474	  sid=$(claude_session "$log")
   475	  transcript=$(claude_transcript "$sid")
   476	  [ -n "$transcript" ] && cp "$transcript" "$work/migrate-claude-session.transcript.jsonl"
   477	  expect "migrate claude: new session answers" '"result":"OK' "$log"
   478	  check "migrate claude: a new session gets one complete $new_version bundle" inspect bundle claude "$transcript" "$new_bundle" exact
   479	}
   480	
   481	migrate_codex() {
   482	  move_main "$old_commit"
   483	  new_codex_home codex-migrate
   484	  local log=$work/migrate-codex.log sid rollout written
   485	  cxc plugin marketplace add "$migrate_url" >"$log" 2>&1
   486	  cxc plugin add adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop >>"$log" 2>&1
   487	  cxc plugin add au-unslop@adhd-unslop >>"$log" 2>&1
   488	  touch "$codex_home/.codex/.adhd-unslop-always"
   489	  codex_plugins "$work/migrate-codex-before.txt"
   490	  check "migrate codex: $old_version installs" has_plugin "$work/migrate-codex-before.txt" adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop "$old_version"
   491	  check "migrate codex: au-unslop is installed" has_plugin "$work/migrate-codex-before.txt" au-unslop@adhd-unslop
   492	
   493	  # Record trust for every 0.2.2 handler, as the /hooks review would.
   494	  written=$(codex_hooks trust adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop 2>>"$log")
   495	  echo "      trusted $written handlers"
   496	  codex_hooks list >"$work/migrate-codex-hooks-before.jsonl" 2>>"$log"
   497	  # shellcheck disable=SC2046
   498	  check "migrate codex: hooks/list reports every $old_version handler trusted" \
   499	    inspect hooks-trusted "$work/migrate-codex-hooks-before.jsonl" adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop "$old_version" $(keys_for adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop "${old_hook_keys[@]}")
   500	
   501	  move_main "$new_commit"
   502	  {
   503	    echo "== marketplace upgrade"
   504	    cxc plugin marketplace upgrade adhd-unslop
   505	    echo "== plugin add"
   506	    cxc plugin add adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop
   507	  } >>"$log" 2>&1
   508	  codex_plugins "$work/migrate-codex-after.txt"
   509	  check "migrate codex: adhd-unslop updates to $new_version" has_plugin "$work/migrate-codex-after.txt" adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop "$new_version"
   510	  check "migrate codex: au-unslop stays installed" has_plugin "$work/migrate-codex-after.txt" au-unslop@adhd-unslop
   511	  codex_hooks list >"$work/migrate-codex-hooks-after.jsonl" 2>>"$log"
   512	  # shellcheck disable=SC2046
   513	  check "migrate codex: the three chunk handlers stay trusted with no review" \
   514	    inspect hooks-trusted "$work/migrate-codex-hooks-after.jsonl" adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop "$new_version" $(keys_for adhd-unslop@adhd-unslop "${new_hook_keys[@]}")
   515	
   516	  # No --dangerously-bypass-hook-trust: only the recorded trust lets the hooks run.
   517	  log=$work/migrate-codex-session.log
   518	  cxe "$log" -o "$work/migrate-codex-session.txt" 'Reply with the single word OK.'
   519	  sid=$(codex_session "$log")
   520	  rollout=$(codex_rollout "$sid")
   521	  [ -n "$rollout" ] && cp "$rollout" "$work/migrate-codex-session.rollout.jsonl"
   522	  expect "migrate codex: new session answers" 'OK' "$work/migrate-codex-session.txt"
   523	  check "migrate codex: exec without the bypass flag gets one complete $new_version bundle" inspect bundle codex "$rollout" "$new_bundle" exact
   524	}
   525	
   526	run_migrate() {
   527	  if ! setup_migrate; then
   528	    result "migrate: local git marketplace starts" no
   529	    echo "      see $work/githttp.log"
   530	    return
   531	  fi
   532	  migrate_claude
   533	  migrate_codex
   534	}
   535	
   536	case $only in
   537	  all) run_claude; run_codex; run_migrate ;;
   538	  claude) run_claude ;;
   539	  codex) run_codex ;;
   540	  migrate) run_migrate ;;
   541	esac
   542	
   543	echo "$pass passed, $fail failed"
   544	[ "$fail" -eq 0 ]
     1	# Working in this repo
     2	
     3	This repo is a Claude Code and Codex plugin marketplace. `adhd-unslop`
     4	combines the i-have-adhd and unslop skills with one tie-breaker and ships
     5	everything it needs. `au-i-have-adhd` and `au-unslop` are optional pinned
     6	mirrors of the two upstream skills. Read `design/DECISIONS.md` before
     7	changing structure. It records why each part is the way it is and the
     8	platform behavior already verified, so nothing needs researching again.
     9	
    10	There is no `CLAUDE.md`. Claude Code reads `AGENTS.md` when no `CLAUDE.md`
    11	exists, so this one file serves both CLIs. Adding a `CLAUDE.md` would hide
    12	this file from Claude Code.
    13	
    14	## Edit sources, never generated files
    15	
    16	- Edit `src/`, `tools/plugins.json`, and `tools/upstream.json`. Every file
    17	  under `plugins/`, and both marketplace files, is generated.
    18	- Run `node tools/build.mjs`, then `node --test tests/*.test.mjs`. CI fails
    19	  on a stale generated file and on any stray file under `plugins/`,
    20	  `.claude-plugin/`, or `.agents/plugins/`. `node tools/build.mjs --prune`
    21	  deletes strays.
    22	- Change upstream text only through `node tools/sync.mjs --bump <name>
    23	  <commit>` or the daily workflow. Never edit `upstream/` by hand.
    24	
    25	## Rules that are easy to break
    26	
    27	- Raise the version in `tools/plugins.json` for every plugin whose shipped
    28	  files change. Installed copies update only on a version change, and the
    29	  version gate in CI fails a pull request that skips it. An overlay edit
    30	  changes `adhd-unslop`.
    31	- Do not change the three chunk handlers in `src/adhd-unslop/hooks/hooks.json`.
    32	  Codex hashes every handler field for hook trust, so any change sends every
    33	  Codex user back to `/hooks`. `tests/hook.test.mjs` compares them with
    34	  `tests/fixtures/trusted-session-start-group.json`. Put behavior changes in
    35	  `always-on.mjs` or `lib.mjs` instead. If a handler must change, update the
    36	  fixture on purpose and say so in the release notes.
    37	- Cite upstream items only as "ADHD rule N", "ADHD exception N", "ADHD
    38	  check N", "unslop rule N", or "unslop process N". `sync.mjs` checks these
    39	  labels against upstream. Other wording escapes the check.
    40	- Keep overlay prose to unslop's rules. `tests/overlay.test.mjs` checks for
    41	  em dashes, curly quotes, title-case headings, and rule 7 words.
    42	- Hooks must exit 0 on every path and never block session start.
    43	- No plugin `dependencies`. A plugin that needs an upstream text embeds it
    44	  with a `{{upstream <name>}}` line in its skill template and lists it in
    45	  `upstreams` in `tools/plugins.json` (DECISIONS D4, D9). Never make a skill
    46	  read rule text from a file: Claude Code prompts for the read and Codex
    47	  often skips it.
    48	- Only `adhd-unslop` has an always-on hook. A second one would inject the
    49	  ADHD rules twice.
    50	- Codex skill names are `$plugin:skill`, such as
    51	  `$adhd-unslop:adhd-unslop`. A bare name does not match. Never suggest a
    52	  `~/.agents/skills` symlink, because it creates a duplicate name that
    53	  Codex refuses to load.
    54	
    55	## Adding a plugin
    56	
    57	1. Add an entry to `tools/plugins.json`: name, version, `"kind":
    58	   "authored"`, category, descriptions, keywords, `codexInterface`, and
    59	   `skills`, with `upstreams` for any upstream a skill embeds.
    60	2. Write `src/<plugin>/skills/<skill>/SKILL.md`, or a `SKILL.md.tmpl` whose
    61	   `{{include <path>}}` lines pull files from `src/<plugin>/` and whose
    62	   `{{upstream <name>}}` lines embed pinned upstream text, plus
    63	   `agents/openai.yaml` for its Codex policy.
    64	3. Run the build and the tests, then `node tools/load-check.mjs`.
    65	
    66	## Testing with the real CLIs
    67	
    68	- `node tools/load-check.mjs` installs the marketplace into throwaway
    69	  Claude Code and Codex homes with no keys and checks what loads. CI runs
    70	  it with the versions pinned in `tools/cli-versions.json`, and daily
    71	  against the latest releases.
    72	- Run `tests/e2e/run.sh` before merging a change to skills, hooks, or
    73	  manifests. It drives the models, so it needs signed-in CLIs.
    74	  `tests/e2e/run.sh migrate` checks the upgrade from 0.2.2.
    75	  `E2E_REPO=<owner/repo> E2E_REF=<branch>` installs from GitHub, which
    76	  tests the real cache paths.
    77	- For any ad hoc test, use a throwaway home: `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=<dir>` for
    78	  Claude Code, and `HOME=<dir> CODEX_HOME=<dir>/.codex` for Codex, with a
    79	  copy of `~/.codex/auth.json` that you delete afterwards. Delete the whole
    80	  Codex home when done: Codex writes the shell environment, API keys
    81	  included, into `$CODEX_HOME/shell_snapshots/`.
    82	- Run the model from a directory away from any marketplace copy. The Codex
    83	  model searches the filesystem and will read stray plugin copies, which
    84	  gives false passes.
    85	- `codex exec` waits on stdin in a non-TTY shell. Always redirect
    86	  `</dev/null`. `codex review` takes no custom prompt, so use `codex exec`.
    87	- `--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust` is for throwaway homes only.
    88	
    89	## Changes and releases
    90	
    91	- Work on a branch and open a PR. `verify.yml` runs the unit tests,
    92	  `build.mjs --check`, the version gate, the keyless load check, and
    93	  workflow linting.
    94	- For a design change, write the proposal under `design/` and run Codex
    95	  adversarial review rounds, `design/round-N-prompt.md` and
    96	  `round-N-codex.md`, until the verdict is CONSENSUS. Record the result in
    97	  `design/DECISIONS.md`.
    98	- Review each `upstream-bump` PR for new conflicts between the two skills.
    99	  Record any new conflict in the outcome table in
   100	  `src/adhd-unslop/overlay/10-precedence.md`.
   101	- After a merge, installed copies update per plugin. The README lists the
   102	  commands for each runtime.

    32	describe("generated files", () => {
    33	  test("every generated file matches a fresh build", () => {
    34	    assert.deepEqual(staleFiles(), []);
    35	  });
    36	
    37	  test("no stray files under generated folders", () => {
    38	    assert.deepEqual(strayFiles(), []);
    39	  });

    41	  test("a stray file is reported, and --prune removes only strays", () => {
    42	    const stray = path.join(repo, "plugins", "adhd-unslop", "skills", "adhd-unslop", "stray-test-file.md");
    43	    fs.writeFileSync(stray, "stray\n");
    44	    try {
    45	      assert.ok(strayFiles().includes("plugins/adhd-unslop/skills/adhd-unslop/stray-test-file.md"));
    46	      const removed = prune();
    47	      assert.deepEqual(removed, ["plugins/adhd-unslop/skills/adhd-unslop/stray-test-file.md"]);
    48	      assert.ok(!fs.existsSync(stray));
    49	      assert.deepEqual(staleFiles(), []);
    50	    } finally {
    51	      fs.rmSync(stray, { force: true });
    52	    }
    53	  });
    54	
    55	  test("no file under plugins/ is a symlink", () => {
    56	    const walk = (dir) => {
    57	      for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    58	        const abs = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    59	        assert.ok(!entry.isSymbolicLink(), `${path.relative(repo, abs)} is a symlink`);
    60	        if (entry.isDirectory()) walk(abs);
    61	      }
    62	    };
    63	    walk(path.join(repo, "plugins"));
    64	  });
    65	
    66	  test("plugins/ holds exactly the plugins in tools/plugins.json", () => {

    68	    assert.deepEqual(dirs, config.plugins.map((p) => p.name).sort());

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# Install this marketplace into throwaway Claude Code and Codex homes and check
     3	# the behavior with the real CLIs. Your own ~/.claude and ~/.codex are not
     4	# touched, except that ~/.codex/auth.json is copied into each throwaway Codex
     5	# home for the run. Every throwaway home, with its auth.json copy and Codex
     6	# shell_snapshots, is deleted on exit. Logs, transcripts, and rollouts stay in
     7	# the printed logs folder.
     8	#
     9	# Needs both CLIs signed in, and node and git, so CI does not run it. Uses the
    10	# working tree, including uncommitted changes. Run `node tools/build.mjs`
    11	# first, so plugins/ is current.
    12	#
    13	#   tests/e2e/run.sh            run everything
    14	#   tests/e2e/run.sh claude     fresh install in Claude Code
    15	#   tests/e2e/run.sh codex      fresh install in Codex
    16	#   tests/e2e/run.sh migrate    upgrade from 0.2.2 in both runtimes
    17	#
    18	# By default the marketplace is a copy of the working tree. To install from
    19	# GitHub instead, which exercises the real clone and cache paths:
    20	#
    21	#   E2E_REPO=rubenvarela/adhd-unslop E2E_REF=main tests/e2e/run.sh
    22	#
    23	# migrate always serves a local git marketplace over smart HTTP on 127.0.0.1.
    24	# Its main branch starts at 0.2.2 (commit fc308bd) and then moves to the
    25	# working tree, or to E2E_REF of E2E_REPO when that is set.
    26	#
    27	# PASS and FAIL lines are checks. INFO lines record platform behavior that is
    28	# kept on purpose or outside this repo's control, and never fail the run.
    29	#
    30	# CLAUDE_BIN and CODEX_BIN pick other CLI binaries, for example a newer Codex:
    31	#
